# Standalone Sales Demo: Meridian Cloud

Run the complete executive-first Sales walkthrough from **this file alone**. It requires no Sales plugin, repository checkout, connectors, package installation, browser automation, or non-standard Python library. The launcher below embeds the complete canonical portfolio and leadership scenarios, provider-shaped evidence, exact conversation templates, dashboard HTML/CSS/JavaScript, renderer, dynamic-date logic, and loopback-only preview server. Generated files live only in the operating system's temporary directory.

## Fast Start: One Command, Then Reply Immediately

When the user asks to run this demo, read this file once, copy its **single Python code block** into a temporary file such as `portable_sales_demo.py`, and run exactly one command:

```text
python3 /tmp/portable_sales_demo.py --step leadership
```

From Codex, perform extraction/file creation and this launch together inside one `functions.exec` call if the code block is not already saved. The command verifies the embedded bundle, materializes runtime-only temporary assets, renders both interactive dashboards, reuses a healthy loopback server or starts one, resolves dates, and prints the exact complete opening. Return the printed Markdown **verbatim as the assistant's final response**, then stop and await the user's numbered reply. Do not first inspect repositories, connectors, browser tabs, installed plugins, other skills, package registries, or dashboard health. Do not invoke `request_user_input`, ask for setup, install plugins, or narrate additional work. A healthy preview is reused on subsequent commands. Target a first response under ten seconds.

If and only if existing `ALL_TOOLS` metadata already exposes callable native Sites create, save-version, and deploy capabilities, append `--sites-available`; do not probe Sites or describe localhost as hosted. Any publication requires the user to select the publication action and separately available Sites tools. Hosting is optional and the complete normal flow requires none.

Append `--available PROVIDER` only for a callable integration still available to authorize or an honestly present recommended plugin, excluding Sites and already-connected providers. Repeat those flags for multiple providers. No verified connection is the honest default. The launcher handles all four combinations, including no available connectors, without claiming access or installing anything.

## Scenario And Evidence Contract

- **Executive:** Maya Chen, Vice President of Sales, Meridian Cloud, North America Enterprise; three regional teams, 38 accounts, 31 opportunities; $13.52M forecast versus a $14.0M target.
- **Executive workspace:** exactly **Forecast & scenarios**, **Top areas of focus**, **Account overview**, and **Seller overview**. The working **Segment** and **Manager** controls change the full visible scope; manager implies its verified region, and unsupported manager-by-segment intersections are labeled estimates.
- **Seller:** Jordan Morgan, Strategic Enterprise Account Executive; ten owned accounts, $3.37M opportunities; 5 work-now, 3 watch, and 2 paused.
- **Northstar Health:** proposed $420,000 expansion; current **Security review** stage, **Best Case** forecast, existing close date, and Jordan Morgan ownership remain unchanged. The 1,200-user pilot **mostly met** engagement, repeat-usage, and deployment goals, but unresolved customer-reported uptime feedback and Casey Patel's administrator-controls questions block a broader rollout.
- **People:** Jordan Lee is Northstar's executive sponsor; Casey Patel is its security/controls reviewer; Priya Shah is Meridian's proposed solutions architect. A proposed ~4,500-user expansion is a separate CRM/planning fact, not an approved rollout or a claim about the unchanged deck.
- **Existing Google Slides deck, exact immutable URL:** https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1JM3bmarfM9neOoTOBtGW12wGUYuYeLqT9HWKdDlMamU/edit?usp=drivesdk
- **Other priorities:** Atlas Manufacturing's $680,000 expansion needs reviewed plant-network performance diagnostics; Solstice Financial's $310,000 opportunity needs delegated-administrator controls verified; Redwood Retail has a $185,000 renewal; Ember Energy and Lattice Public Sector remain paused.
- **Date tokens:** prior business days skip weekends, the decision checkpoint is the next future Thursday, and the fiscal year follows the current calendar year. Never expose unresolved `{{...}}` placeholders.
- **Provider-shaped sources are fictional embedded evidence**, never live reads: Salesforce, Granola, Gmail, Google Calendar, Slack, Google Drive, and approved GTM-resource-hub records. Show source attribution tables only in the executive and seller introductions; the optional deck uses a single concise source sentence, and the completed meeting repeats no source inventory.

## Skill Configuration

### Source Resolution

This Markdown document is the entire portable source of truth. Its human-readable flow below owns user-facing state, exact templates, menus, branches, review gates, and return behavior. Its embedded launcher contains 19 canonical scripts, HTML templates, JSON datasets, and Markdown evidence files. No path in the source checkout, installed plugin, adjacent skill, connector, hosted application, or external package is required.

### Safety And Interaction Boundaries

1. Disclose fictional data **once**, in the opening response. Remain in character thereafter; at completion explain exactly once that the approved Salesforce update was simulated and that **no external Salesforce record was changed**.
2. Keep only conversational state: active persona, active state, offered optional publication, current dashboard URL, interrupted branch, and the exact reviewed proposal. Do not persist workflow state.
3. Every nonterminal response is a normal assistant final message with at most three numbered choices. Stop after each response. Invalid choices repeat the current menu; free-form questions get grounded answers and restore the interrupted menu. Never open structured input.
4. Executive first, then seller, then optional unchanged deck/outreach, then completed-meeting review. Do not auto-advance. Preserve the existing exact deck link and never copy, generate, edit, send, or publish a replacement.
5. Present only four changed opportunity fields: **Next Step**, **Deal Notes**, **Decision Criteria & Purchase Process**, and **Risks and Asks**. Preserve amount, stage, close date, forecast, and owner. The review says nothing was applied and offers **1. Save it to Salesforce** first. A changed proposal needs fresh approval.
6. Selecting the reviewed save authorizes only a **simulated** result. Never call Salesforce, Gmail, Calendar, Slack, Drive, transcript services, Sites, or other external connectors to populate this demo. Never create or send email, schedule a meeting, create CRM tasks, change records, install plugins, or publish artifacts without distinct explicit authorization.
7. Avoid invented outages, uptime percentages, incident counts, SLA breaches, technical causes, guaranteed fixes, security sign-off, approved expansion, release dates, staffing assignments, booked meetings, or committed forecast upgrades.
8. The terminal setup invitation distinguishes verified authenticated connections from merely callable integrations and installable recommendations. Do not count Sites as a sales connector or install anything automatically.

- **Additional free-form question:** Explain the supporting call, existing deck, approved account source, relevant GTM context, or unresolved rollout decision, then restore the presentation menu.
- **Output boundaries:** Show a concise source-evidence sentence before the exact Google Slides link, keep the presentation description concise, and list at most three narrative uncertainties and three numbered next actions. Never expose private deal-desk messages, unapproved services, internal staffing, unsupported case-study outcomes, an invented uptime incident or SLA, a guaranteed fix, a booked meeting, or an approved expansion.

- **Parent:** The executive leadership dashboard only.

Load the available Sites building and Sites hosting skills, use the matching native Sites creation/save/deployment tools, publish only the reviewed executive leadership dashboard, retain its single understated footer disclosure, and use appropriately restricted workspace access when supported. Selecting this exact executive publication option authorizes publication of that artifact only; do not publish anything before that selection.
- **Failure:** If Sites is no longer callable, access is blocked, publishing fails, or the dashboard contains content that should not be shared, explain the limitation, keep the working localhost link, and return to the remaining executive actions without claiming publication succeeded or offering publication again.
- **Success:** Present the real verified hosted executive URL, explain who can open it, then offer only the remaining executive actions; never repeat publication or offer to publish the seller dashboard.

```python
AAAAAAAAAACAAePYAQByZWZlcmVuY2VzL2RlbW8tZmxvdy5tZFBLBQYAAAAAEwATAOIFAABIAgIAAAA=
"""


def materialize() -> Path:
    """Extract embedded fixtures/templates/scripts into a reusable temporary folder."""
    raw = base64.b64decode(BUNDLE_BASE64)
    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != BUNDLE_SHA256:
        raise RuntimeError("The embedded Sales demo bundle failed its integrity check.")
    root = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"portable-sales-demo-{BUNDLE_SHA256[:16]}"
    marker = root / ".bundle-sha256"
    if marker.exists() and marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() == BUNDLE_SHA256:
        return root
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as archive:
        for entry in archive.infolist():
            destination = (root / entry.filename).resolve()
            if not destination.is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
                raise RuntimeError("An embedded Sales demo path escaped its temporary directory.")
            destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            destination.write_bytes(archive.read(entry))
    marker.write_text(BUNDLE_SHA256, encoding="utf-8")
    return root


def load_renderer(root: Path):
    renderer_path = root / "scripts" / "render_demo_dashboard.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("portable_sales_demo_renderer", renderer_path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError("Could not import the embedded dashboard renderer.")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def names(values: list[str]) -> str:
    values = list(dict.fromkeys(value.strip() for value in values if value.strip()))
    if len(values) < 2:
        return values[0] if values else ""
    if len(values) == 2:
        return f"{values[0]} and {values[1]}"
    return f"{', '.join(values[:-1])}, and {values[-1]}"


def terminal_response(connected: list[str], available: list[str]) -> str:
    connected = list(dict.fromkeys(value.strip() for value in connected if value.strip()))
    available = list(
        dict.fromkeys(value.strip() for value in available if value.strip() and value not in connected)
    )
    connected_line = (
        f"- **Verified connected:** {names(connected)}."
        if connected
        else "- **Verified connected:** None; no sales connector account has been verified."
    )
    available_line = (
        f"- **Available to connect or add:** {names(available)}; installation and account authorization are separate."
        if available
        else "- **Available to connect or add:** No additional compatible sales connector has been verified as available."
    )
    if connected and available:
        invitation = (
            f"I found that {names(connected)} {'is' if len(connected) == 1 else 'are'} already connected "
            f"and {names(available)} {'is' if len(available) == 1 else 'are'} available. "
            "Would you like to set those up so we can try these flows on your data?"
        )
    elif connected:
        invitation = (
            f"Would you like to try these sales flows on your own data using "
            f"your verified {names(connected)} {'connection' if len(connected) == 1 else 'connections'}?"
        )
    elif available:
        invitation = (
            "I haven't verified any sales connectors as connected yet, but "
            f"{names(available)} {'is' if len(available) == 1 else 'are'} available. "
            "Would you like to set those up so we can try these flows on your data?"
        )
    else:
        invitation = (
            "I haven't verified a connected or available sales integration yet. "
            "Would you like help finding a compatible sales connector so we can try these flows on your data?"
        )
    return "\n".join(
        [
            "**Northstar follow-up completed**",
            "",
            "**Salesforce update: simulated after your approval.** The reviewed next step, meeting "
            "notes, decision criteria, and risk capture the mostly-met pilot goals, Jordan's follow-up "
            "commitment, unresolved customer uptime feedback, and Casey's supporting controls review.",
            "",
            "The $420,000 value, Security review stage, close date, Best Case forecast category, and "
            "account owner remain unchanged. **No external Salesforce record was changed.**",
            "",
            "**Sales integrations in your environment**",
            "",
            connected_line,
            available_line,
            "- Exposed capabilities and packaged scenario evidence do not establish account access.",
            "",
            "That completes the leadership overview, seller account home, reviewed meeting follow-up, "
            "and Salesforce approval flow.",
            "",
            f"**Next step:** {invitation}",
        ]
    )


def branch_response(step: str) -> str:
    if step == "risk":
        return (
            "**Biggest forecast risk**\n\n"
            "The division is forecast at $13.52M against a $14.0M target: a $480K gap. "
            "Northstar's $420K expansion remains in Best Case because its 1,200-user pilot mostly "
            "met its original goals, but customer-reported uptime feedback and Casey Patel's controls "
            "questions are unresolved. Atlas adds a $680K performance-validation risk; Solstice's "
            "$310K opportunity still needs delegated-administrator controls verified. None should be "
            "promoted on seller optimism.\n\n"
            "After you've opened your exec dashboard, I'd suggest:\n\n"
            "1. Next demo: seller account overview\n"
            "2. Explain the biggest forecast risk\n"
            "3. Inspect the top leadership decision"
        )
    if step == "decision":
        return (
            "**Top leadership decision**\n\n"
            "Ask Jordan Morgan and solutions architect Priya Shah to review Northstar's existing "
            "pilot scorecard and customer-reported uptime concern with Jordan Lee and Casey Patel. "
            "Clarify the operational impact and remaining administrator-controls questions before "
            "the customer decides whether a broader rollout is justified. Do not promise a fix, "
            "meeting, staffing commitment, rollout, or forecast upgrade.\n\n"
            "After you've opened your exec dashboard, I'd suggest:\n\n"
            "1. Next demo: seller account overview\n"
            "2. Explain the biggest forecast risk\n"
            "3. Inspect the top leadership decision"
        )
    if step == "rules":
        return (
            "**Salesforce update rules**\n\n"
            "Salesforce owns the current opportunity, owner, amount, stage, close date, and forecast; "
            "the completed customer meeting supports only the reviewed Next Step, Deal Notes, "
            "Decision Criteria & Purchase Process, and Risks and Asks. Keep the $420,000 amount, "
            "Security review stage, existing close date, Best Case forecast, and Jordan Morgan's "
            "ownership unchanged. The exact four-field proposal must be reviewed and explicitly "
            "approved before even the simulated save; any changed proposal requires fresh approval.\n\n"
            "Next steps\n\n"
            "1. Save it to Salesforce\n"
            "2. Explain the CRM update rules\n"
            "3. Return to the account overview"
        )
    if step == "validate":
        return (
            "**What Northstar still needs to validate**\n\n"
            "Confirm whether the 1,200-user pilot sufficiently met its engagement, repeat-usage, "
            "and deployment-milestone goals; clarify the reported uptime feedback and observed "
            "operational impact; and resolve Casey Patel's supporting administrator-controls "
            "questions before Jordan Lee determines whether a broader rollout should be evaluated. "
            "No outage count, SLA breach, cause, fix, expansion approval, or rollout date is known.\n\n"
            "After reviewing your Northstar presentation, you can:\n\n"
            "1. Draft the outreach email giving Jordan Lee a heads-up about the pilot-readiness sync\n"
            "2. See the next demo: Following up after the Northstar meeting\n"
            "3. Explain what still needs to be validated before the pilot can scale"
        )
    raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown deterministic branch: {step}")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--step",
        choices=(
            "leadership", "account", "meeting", "presentation", "email", "complete", "risk",
            "decision", "rules", "validate",
        ),
        default="leadership",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", help="An existing healthy loopback preview to reuse.")
    parser.add_argument("--sites-available", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--connected", action="append", default=[], metavar="VERIFIED_PROVIDER")
    parser.add_argument("--available", action="append", default=[], metavar="AVAILABLE_PROVIDER")
    parser.add_argument("--print-runtime-root", action="store_true")
    options = parser.parse_args()
    root = materialize()
    if options.print_runtime_root:
        print(root)
        return
    if options.step == "complete":
        print(terminal_response(options.connected, options.available), flush=True)
        return
    if options.step in {"risk", "decision", "rules", "validate"}:
        print(branch_response(options.step), flush=True)
        return
    sys.argv = [str(root / "scripts/start_demo_fast.py"), "--step", options.step]
    if options.base_url:
        sys.argv.extend(["--base-url", options.base_url])
    if options.sites_available:
        sys.argv.append("--sites-available")
    runpy.run_path(str(root / "scripts/start_demo_fast.py"), run_name="__main__")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### Portable Validation Checklist

- `python3 portable_sales_demo.py --step leadership` prints the complete Maya introduction and a live `/leadership/` URL without package installs or connectors.
- `--step account` prints Jordan's introduction and serves the matching ten-account dashboard at `/`.
- `--step presentation` preserves the exact existing Google Slides URL; `--step email` creates only an in-chat review draft.
- `--step meeting` contains exactly four diff rows, no repeated source table, and `1. Save it to Salesforce` as its first action.
- `--step complete --available Gmail --available "Google Calendar"` reports a simulated save, preserves protected commercial fields, and ends with an honest personalized connector-setup question.
- Executive Segment and Manager controls re-render all four sections, scenario dates are current, and no user-facing response includes unresolved dynamic placeholders.
